# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title joseph Model

class DirectoryFilter {
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    isIgnored(path : String) : boolean
    isIgnoredPath(path : Path) : boolean
}
class DirectoryToMarkdown {
    directoryFilter : DirectoryFilter
    fileContentReader : FileContentReader
    markdownWriter : MarkdownWriter
    convert(options : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions) : MarkdownConversionResult
    createDefaultIgnoreDirs() : List<String>
    createDefaultOptions() : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath : Path, extension : String, content : String) : MarkdownFileEntry
    createOptions(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    directoryToMarkdown(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : int
    getExtension(path : Path) : String
    isIgnored(path : String, ignoreDirs : List<String>) : boolean
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String) : String
    readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPath(filePath : Path) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
    walkDirectory(currentDir : Path, md : BufferedWriter, optons : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, baseDir : Path, result : MarkdownConversionResult) : void
}
class DirectoryToMarkdownOptions {
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

Algorithm:
  if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
  if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
  currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
  if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
  entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
  for each entry in entries:
      if entry is a directory:
          if optons.recursive:
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
      else if entry is a regular file:
          rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
          if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
          ext = getExtension(entry);
          if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
          readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
          if !readResult.readable, continue;
          fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
          if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryFilter::isIgnored
Summary: Decides whether a given path string is located inside one of the ignored directories.
Algorithm:
  if path is null or blank, or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false;
  convert path to Path and delegate to isIgnoredPath(Path);
  return the result.
Related Features: ignoreDirs, isIgnoredPath
Related Classes: None
Input: path - a relative or absolute path string to a file or directory.
Output: boolean - true if any path component exactly equals an ignored directory name; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: path is a valid path string, or null/blank for which false is returned.
Post-condition: The result is based on exact path-component matching, not substring matching.

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
Algorithm:
  if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
  if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
  currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
  if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
  entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
  for each entry in entries:
      if entry is a directory:
          if optons.recursive:
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
      else if entry is a regular file:
          rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
          if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
          ext = getExtension(entry);
          if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
          readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
          if !readResult.readable, continue;
          fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
          if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, and optons are non-null; md is open and writable.
Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Determines whether a file extension should be included based on the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  if fileTypes == null or fileTypes.isEmpty(), return true;
  if extension == null, return false;
  ext = extension.toLowerCase();
  for each type in fileTypes:
      if type != null and type.toLowerCase().equals(ext), return true;
  return false;
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension - lowercase file extension including leading dot, e.g. ".txt"; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for no filter.
Output: boolean - true if the extension is accepted.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null, or false is returned when a filter exists.
Post-condition: An empty or null filter admits all extensions; otherwise comparison is case-insensitive and requires exact full extension matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Converts the configured directory into a Markdown file and returns a result that tracks all processed files.
Algorithm:
  if options is null, options = createDefaultOptions();
  if options.ignoreDirs is null, options.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  result = new MarkdownConversionResult();
  baseDir = Paths.get(options.directoryPath);
  outputPath = Paths.get(options.outputFile);
  if baseDir does not exist or is not a directory:
      create/truncate outputPath and return result;
  try (BufferedWriter md = Files.newBufferedWriter(outputPath, UTF_8)) {
      walkDirectory(baseDir, md, options, baseDir, result);
  }
  return result;
Related Features: walkDirectory, createDefaultOptions, createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions containing root directory, output file, file type filters, ignored directories, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles list.
Pre-condition: options is non-null, or will default; directoryPath and outputFile strings are non-null; output file location is writable; maxFileSizeMb <= 0 is treated as no size limit.
Post-condition: The output file is created/overwritten with the generated Markdown document; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); if no files are eligible, the output file is empty.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry object for one successfully read file.
Algorithm:
  ext = extension == null ? "" : extension;
  language = ext.startsWith(".") ? ext.substring(1) : ext;
  relative = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');
  return new MarkdownFileEntry(relative, ext, language, content);
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath - Path of the file relative to the root directory; extension - lowercase file extension including the leading dot, or empty; content - UTF-8 text content of the file.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry containing relativePath, extension, language (extension without the leading dot), and content.
Pre-condition: relativePath is non-null; extension is non-null; content may be empty but is non-null.
Post-condition: The returned entry has language derived from extension and a Markdown-portable relative path using '/' separators.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a UTF-8 text file by Path, returning its content only if it is readable and within the size limit.
Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return null;
  FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(filePath, maxSizeMb);
  return r.readable ? r.content : null;
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

```plantuml
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>

    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}

    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}

    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}

    processedFileCount : int

}

    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}

}

    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
Algorithm:
  if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
  if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
  currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
  if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
  entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
  for each entry in entries:
      if entry is a directory:
          if optons.recursive:
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
      else if entry is a regular file:
          rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
          if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
          ext = getExtension(entry);
          if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
          readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
          if !readResult.readable, continue;
          fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
          if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, and optons are non-null; md is open and writable.
Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Determines whether a file extension should be included based on the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  if fileTypes == null or fileTypes.isEmpty(), return true;
  if extension == null, return false;
  ext = extension.toLowerCase();
  for each type in fileTypes:
      if type != null and type.toLowerCase().equals(ext), return true;
  return false;
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension - lowercase file extension including leading dot, e.g. ".txt"; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for no filter.
Output: boolean - true if the extension is accepted.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null, or false is returned when a filter exists.
Post-condition: An empty or null filter admits all extensions; otherwise comparison is case-insensitive and requires exact full extension matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Converts the configured directory into a Markdown file and returns a result that tracks all processed files.
Algorithm:
  if options is null, options = createDefaultOptions();
  if options.ignoreDirs is null, options.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  result = new MarkdownConversionResult();
  baseDir = Paths.get(options.directoryPath);
  outputPath = Paths.get(options.outputFile);
  if baseDir does not exist or is not a directory:
      create/truncate outputPath and return result;
  try (BufferedWriter md = Files.newBufferedWriter(outputPath, UTF_8)) {
      walkDirectory(baseDir, md, options, baseDir, result);
  }
  return result;
Related Features: walkDirectory, createDefaultOptions, createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions containing root directory, output file, file type filters, ignored directories, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles list.
Pre-condition: options is non-null, or will default; directoryPath and outputFile strings are non-null; output file location is writable; maxFileSizeMb <= 0 is treated as no size limit.
Post-condition: The output file is created/overwritten with the generated Markdown document; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); if no files are eligible, the output file is empty.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry object for one successfully read file.
Algorithm:
  ext = extension == null ? "" : extension;
  language = ext.startsWith(".") ? ext.substring(1) : ext;
  relative = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');
  return new MarkdownFileEntry(relative, ext, language, content);
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath - Path of the file relative to the root directory; extension - lowercase file extension including the leading dot, or empty; content - UTF-8 text content of the file.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry containing relativePath, extension, language (extension without the leading dot), and content.
Pre-condition: relativePath is non-null; extension is non-null; content may be empty but is non-null.
Post-condition: The returned entry has language derived from extension and a Markdown-portable relative path using '/' separators.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a UTF-8 text file by Path, returning its content only if it is readable and within the size limit.

  return r.readable ? r.content : null;

```text
Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Class Diagram

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
Algorithm:
  if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
  if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
  if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
  currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
  if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
  entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
  for each entry in entries:
      if entry is a directory:
          if optons.recursive:
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
      else if entry is a regular file:
          rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
          if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
          ext = getExtension(entry);
          if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
          readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
          if !readResult.readable, continue;
          fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
          if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, and directoryFilter are non-null; md is open and writable.
Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary: Determines whether a file extension should be included based on the configured file type filter.
Algorithm:
  if fileTypes == null or fileTypes.isEmpty(), return true;
  if extension == null, return false;
  ext = extension.toLowerCase();
  for each type in fileTypes:
      if type != null and type.toLowerCase().equals(ext), return true;
  return false;
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: extension - lowercase file extension including leading dot, e.g. ".txt"; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for no filter.
Output: boolean - true if the extension is accepted.
Pre-condition: extension is non-null, or false is returned when a filter exists.
Post-condition: An empty or null filter admits all extensions; otherwise comparison is case-insensitive and requires exact full extension matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary: Converts the configured directory into a Markdown file and returns a result that tracks all processed files.
Algorithm:
  if options is null, options = createDefaultOptions();
  if options.ignoreDirs is null, options.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  result = new MarkdownConversionResult();
  baseDir = Paths.get(options.directoryPath);
  outputPath = Paths.get(options.outputFile);
  if baseDir does not exist or is not a directory:
      create/truncate outputPath and return result;
  try (BufferedWriter md = Files.newBufferedWriter(outputPath, UTF_8)) {
      walkDirectory(baseDir, md, options, baseDir, result);
  }
  return result;
Related Features: walkDirectory, createDefaultOptions, createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: options - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions containing root directory, output file, file type filters, ignored directories, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
Output: MarkdownConversionResult - contains processedFileCount and processedFiles list.
Pre-condition: options is non-null, or will default; directoryPath and outputFile strings are non-null; output file location is writable; maxFileSizeMb <= 0 is treated as no size limit.
Post-condition: The output file is created/overwritten with the generated Markdown document; result.processedFileCount equals result.processedFiles.size(); if no files are eligible, the output file is empty.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry object for one successfully read file.
Algorithm:
  ext = extension == null ? "" : extension;
  language = ext.startsWith(".") ? ext.substring(1) : ext;
  relative = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');
  return new MarkdownFileEntry(relative, ext, language, content);
Related Features: None
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input: relativePath - Path of the file relative to the root directory; extension - lowercase file extension including the leading dot, or empty; content - UTF-8 text content of the file.
Output: MarkdownFileEntry containing relativePath, extension, language (extension without the leading dot), and content.
Pre-condition: relativePath is non-null; extension is non-null; content may be empty but is non-null.
Post-condition: The returned entry has language derived from extension and a Markdown-portable relative path using '/' separators.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary: Reads a UTF-8 text file by Path, returning its content only if it is readable and within the size limit.
Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return null;
  FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(filePath, maxSizeMb);
  return r.readable ? r.content : null;
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath - Path to a regular file; maxSizeMb - maximum size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
Output: String - file content if readable and not too large; null if the file is skipped.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null, or null is returned; maxSizeMb is any int.
Post-condition: The returned string is the UTF-8 decoded content; skipped files return null and do not provide a skip reason.

DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary: Checks whether any path component of a given path string is present in an explicit ignore list.
Algorithm:
  if path is null/blank or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false;
  normalized = path.replace('\\', '/');
  components = normalized.split("/");
  for each component:
      if ignoreDirs.contains(component), return true;
  return false;
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path - relative or absolute path string; ignoreDirs - list of directory names to ignore.
Output: boolean - true if any component exactly equals an entry in ignoreDirs; false otherwise.
Pre-condition: ignoreDirs contains no null entries.
Post-condition: Matching is by path component, not by substring; empty or invalid path strings are not ignored.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object from explicit configuration values.
Algorithm:
  if directoryPath == null or outputFile == null, throw IllegalArgumentException;
  if ignoreDirs == null, ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  if fileTypes == null, fileTypes = empty list;
  return new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions(
      directoryPath.toString(),
      outputFile.toString(),
      new ArrayList<>(fileTypes),
      new ArrayList<>(ignoreDirs),
      recursive,
      maxFileSizeMb
  );
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: directoryPath - root directory Path; outputFile - output Markdown file Path; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for all files; ignoreDirs - list of ignored directory names, or null for defaults; recursive - whether nested directories are scanned; maxFileSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 means no size limit.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with the corresponding values.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null.
Post-condition: The returned options object contains copies of the input lists so later external mutation does not affect it.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience method that builds options, converts a directory to Markdown, and returns only the processed file count.
Algorithm:
  options = createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb);
  result = convert(options);
  return result.processedFileCount;
Related Features: createOptions, convert
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath - root directory Path; outputFile - output Markdown file Path; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for all files; ignoreDirs - list of ignored directory names, or null for defaults; recursive - whether nested directories are scanned; maxFileSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 means no size limit.
Output: int - number of successfully processed files.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; output file location is writable.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of processed files recorded in the conversion result.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary: Reads a UTF-8 text file by string path, returning its content only if it is readable and within the size limit.
Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return null;
  FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb);
  return r.readable ? r.content : null;
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath - path string to a regular file; maxSizeMb - maximum size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
Output: String - file content if readable and not too large; null if the file is skipped.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null, or null is returned; maxSizeMb is any int.
Post-condition: The returned string is the UTF-8 decoded content; skipped files return null and do not provide a skip reason.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object populated with default configuration values.
Algorithm:
  return new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions(
      directoryPath = ".",
      outputFile = "output.md",
      fileTypes = empty list,
      ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs(),
      recursive = false,
      maxFileSizeMb = 10
  );
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input: none.
Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with default root ".", default output "output.md", no file type filter, default ignored directories, non-recursive traversal, and a default maximum file size of 10 MB.
Pre-condition: none.
Post-condition: A new, independent options object is returned; no global state is changed.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary: Returns the lowercase file extension of a path, including the leading dot.
Algorithm:
  if path == null or path.getFileName() == null, return "";
  name = path.getFileName().toString();
  lastDot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
  if lastDot < 0 || lastDot == 0 || lastDot == name.length()-1, return "";
  return name.substring(lastDot).toLowerCase();
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path - file or directory Path.
Output: String - extension with leading dot, lowercased; empty string if no extension is present.
Pre-condition: path is non-null, or empty string is returned.
Post-condition: The result does not include the file name before the last dot; hidden dotfiles such as ".gitignore" have no extension.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
Algorithm:
  if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
  if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
  if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
  directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
  currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
  if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;

  for each entry in entries:
      if entry is a directory:
          if optons.recursive:
              walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);

          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));

Post-condition: The returned options object contains copies of the input lists so later external mutation does not affect it.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary: Convenience method that builds options, converts a directory to Markdown, and returns only the processed file count.
Algorithm:
  options = createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb);
  result = convert(options);
  return result.processedFileCount;
Related Features: createOptions, convert
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
Input: directoryPath - root directory Path; outputFile - output Markdown file Path; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for all files; ignoreDirs - list of ignored directory names, or null for defaults; recursive - whether nested directories are scanned; maxFileSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 means no size limit.
Output: int - number of successfully processed files.
Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; output file location is writable.
Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of processed files recorded in the conversion result.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb

Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return null;
  FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb);
  return r.readable ? r.content : null;
Related Features: fileContentReader
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
Input: filePath - path string to a regular file; maxSizeMb - maximum size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
Output: String - file content if readable and not too large; null if the file is skipped.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null, or null is returned; maxSizeMb is any int.
Post-condition: The returned string is the UTF-8 decoded content; skipped files return null and do not provide a skip reason.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object populated with default configuration values.

      directoryPath = ".",

      fileTypes = empty list,

      maxFileSizeMb = 10
  );
Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions

Pre-condition: none.
Post-condition: A new, independent options object is returned; no global state is changed.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary: Returns the lowercase file extension of a path, including the leading dot.
Algorithm:
  if path == null or path.getFileName() == null, return "";
  name = path.getFileName().toString();
  lastDot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
  if lastDot < 0 || lastDot == 0 || lastDot == name.length()-1, return "";
  return name.substring(lastDot).toLowerCase();
Related Features: None
Related Classes: None
Input: path - file or directory Path.
Output: String - extension with leading dot, lowercased; empty string if no extension is present.
Pre-condition: path is non-null, or empty string is returned.
Post-condition: The result does not include the file name before the last dot; hidden dotfiles such as ".gitignore" have no extension.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
FileContentReader::readFileSafely
Summary: Safely reads a file with a size limit and returns a result containing content and a skip reason.
Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return FileReadResult("", false, UNREADABLE);
  if maxSizeMb > 0:
      if !Files.isRegularFile(filePath), return FileReadResult("", false, UNREADABLE);
      if Files.size(filePath) > (long)maxSizeMb * 1024 * 1024, return FileReadResult("", false, TOO_LARGE);
  try:
      bytes = Files.readAllBytes(filePath);
      content = new String(bytes, UTF_8);
      return FileReadResult(content, true, NONE);
  catch (IOException e):
      return FileReadResult("", false, UNREADABLE);
Related Features: None
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath - Path to a file; maxSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
Output: FileReadResult - if readable, content is the UTF-8 decoded text and skippedReason is NONE; if too large, readable is false and skippedReason is TOO_LARGE; if unreadable/non-regular, readable is false and skippedReason is UNREADABLE.
Pre-condition: filePath is non-null, or an UNREADABLE result is returned; maxSizeMb is any int.
Post-condition: Files larger than the limit are not read; unreadable files do not throw; the returned result contains the correct skip reason.

FileContentReader::readFileSafelyFromString
Summary: Safely reads a file from a string path with a size limit and returns a FileReadResult.
Algorithm:
  if filePath == null, return FileReadResult("", false, UNREADABLE);
  try:
      return readFileSafely(Paths.get(filePath), maxSizeMb);
  catch (InvalidPathException e):
      return FileReadResult("", false, UNREADABLE);
Related Features: readFileSafely
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input: filePath - path string to a file; maxSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
Output: FileReadResult - same semantics as readFileSafely.
Pre-condition: maxSizeMb is any int; filePath may be null for which UNREADABLE is returned.
Post-condition: The result is the same as calling readFileSafely with Paths.get(filePath).

      return new FileReadResult("", false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
  }
  try {
      if (!Files.isRegularFile(filePath)) {
          return new FileReadResult("", false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
      }
      if (maxSizeMb > 0 && Files.size(filePath) > (long) maxSizeMb * 1024 * 1024) {
          return new FileReadResult("", false, SkipReason.TOO_LARGE);
      }
      byte[] bytes = Files.readAllBytes(filePath);
      String content = new String(bytes, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
      return new FileReadResult(content, true, SkipReason.NONE);
  } catch (IOException e) {
      return new FileReadResult("", false, SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
  }

      result.setReadable(true);

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry

   * Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
   * Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, directoryFilter, fileContentReader, and markdownWriter are non-null; md is open and writable.
   * Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.
```

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
```

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
   * Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
   * Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, directoryFilter, fileContentReader, and markdownWriter are non-null; md is open and writable.
   * Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
```

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
   * Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
   * Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, directoryFilter, fileContentReader, and markdownWriter are non-null; md is open and writable.
   * Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
```

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
   * Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
   * Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, directoryFilter, fileContentReader, and markdownWriter are non-null; md is open and writable.
   * Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated
```

      opts.getIgnoreDirs().addAll(createDefaultIgnoreDirs());
    }
    MarkdownConversionResult result = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createMarkdownConversionResult();
    Path baseDir = Paths.get(opts.getDirectoryPath());
    Path outputPath = Paths.get(opts.getOutputFile());

    if (!Files.exists(baseDir) || !Files.isDirectory(baseDir))
    {
      try
      {
        Path parent = outputPath.getParent();
        if (parent != null)
        {
          Files.createDirectories(parent);
        }
        Files.write(outputPath, new byte[0], StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.TRUNCATE_EXISTING);
      }
      catch (IOException e)
      {
        // Ignore: if the output cannot be created, return an empty result.
      }
      return result;
    }

    try (BufferedWriter md = Files.newBufferedWriter(outputPath, StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
    {
      walkDirectory(baseDir, md, opts, baseDir, result);
    }
    catch (IOException e)
    {
      throw new RuntimeException(e);
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Checks whether any path component of a given path string is present in an explicit ignore list.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if path is null/blank or ignoreDirs is null/empty, return false;
   *   normalized = path.replace('\\', '/');
   *   components = normalized.split("/");
   *   for each component:
   *       if ignoreDirs.contains(component), return true;
   *   return false;
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: path - relative or absolute path string; ignoreDirs - list of directory names to ignore.
   * Output: boolean - true if any component exactly equals an entry in ignoreDirs; false otherwise.
   * Pre-condition: ignoreDirs contains no null entries.
   * Post-condition: Matching is by path component, not by substring; empty or invalid path strings are not ignored.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model ignoreDirsMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isIgnored(final String path, final EList<String> ignoreDirs)
  {
    if (path == null || path.trim().isEmpty() || ignoreDirs == null || ignoreDirs.isEmpty())
    {
      return false;
    }
    String normalized = path.replace('\\', '/');
    String[] components = normalized.split("/");
    for (String component : components)
    {
      if (ignoreDirs.contains(component))
      {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Reads a UTF-8 text file by string path, returning its content only if it is readable and within the size limit.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if filePath == null, return null;
   *   FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb);

   * Related Features: fileContentReader
   * Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult
   * Input: filePath - path string to a regular file; maxSizeMb - maximum size in MB, <=0 disables the size limit.
   * Output: String - file content if readable and within the size limit; null if the file is skipped. This convenience method does not expose the SkipReason; callers needing the skip reason should use FileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString.
   * Pre-condition: filePath may be null; if non-null, it is a path string to a regular file; fileContentReader is non-null; maxSizeMb is any int.

  public String readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(final String filePath, final int maxSizeMb)
  {

    {
      return null;
    }
    FileReadResult r = fileContentReader.readFileSafelyFromString(filePath, maxSizeMb);
    return r.isReadable() ? r.getContent() : null;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Convenience method that builds options, converts a directory to Markdown, and returns only the processed file count.
   * Algorithm:
   *   options = createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb);
   *   result = convert(options);
   *   return result.processedFileCount;
   * Related Features: createOptions, convert
   * Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult
   * Input: directoryPath - root directory Path; outputFile - output Markdown file Path; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for all files; ignoreDirs - list of ignored directory names, or null for defaults; recursive - whether nested directories are scanned; maxFileSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 means no size limit.
   * Output: int - number of successfully processed files.
   * Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null; output file location is writable.
   * Post-condition: The Markdown output file is written; the returned count equals the number of processed files recorded in the conversion result.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model directoryPathDataType="edu.joseph.Path" outputFileDataType="edu.joseph.Path" fileTypesMany="true" ignoreDirsMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int directoryToMarkdown(final Path directoryPath, final Path outputFile, final EList<String> fileTypes, final EList<String> ignoreDirs, final boolean recursive, final int maxFileSizeMb)
  {
    DirectoryToMarkdownOptions options = createOptions(directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, maxFileSizeMb);
    MarkdownConversionResult result = convert(options);
    return result.getProcessedFileCount();
  }

  public EList<String> createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
  {
    EList<String> result = new BasicEList<String>();
    result.add("node_modules");
    result.add("__pycache__");
    result.add(".git");
    result.add(".vscode");
    result.add(".idea");
    return result;
  }

   * Pre-condition: none.
