  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions object from explicit configuration values.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if directoryPath == null or outputFile == null, throw IllegalArgumentException;
   *   if ignoreDirs == null, ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   if fileTypes == null, fileTypes = empty list;
   *   return new DirectoryToMarkdownOptions(
   *       directoryPath.toString(),
   *       outputFile.toString(),
   *       new ArrayList<>(fileTypes),
   *       new ArrayList<>(ignoreDirs),
   *       recursive,
   *       maxFileSizeMb
   *   );
   * Related Features: createDefaultIgnoreDirs
   * Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
   * Input: directoryPath - root directory Path; outputFile - output Markdown file Path; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for all files; ignoreDirs - list of ignored directory names, or null for defaults; recursive - whether nested directories are scanned; maxFileSizeMb - maximum file size in MB, <=0 means no size limit.
   * Output: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions with the corresponding values.
   * Pre-condition: directoryPath and outputFile are non-null.
   * Post-condition: The returned options object contains copies of the input lists so later external mutation does not affect it.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model directoryPathDataType="edu.joseph.Path" outputFileDataType="edu.joseph.Path" fileTypesMany="true" ignoreDirsMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public DirectoryToMarkdownOptions createOptions(final Path directoryPath, final Path outputFile, final EList<String> fileTypes, final EList<String> ignoreDirs, final boolean recursive, final int maxFileSizeMb)
  {
    if (directoryPath == null || outputFile == null)
    {
      throw new IllegalArgumentException();
    }
    EList<String> effectiveFileTypes = fileTypes;
    if (effectiveFileTypes == null)
    {
      effectiveFileTypes = new BasicEList<String>();
    }
    EList<String> effectiveIgnoreDirs = ignoreDirs;
    if (effectiveIgnoreDirs == null)
    {
      effectiveIgnoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
    }
    DirectoryToMarkdownOptions options = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createDirectoryToMarkdownOptions();
    options.setDirectoryPath(directoryPath.toString());
    options.setOutputFile(outputFile.toString());
    options.getFileTypes().addAll(effectiveFileTypes);
    options.getIgnoreDirs().addAll(effectiveIgnoreDirs);
    options.setRecursive(recursive);
    options.setMaxFileSizeMb(maxFileSizeMb);
    return options;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Recursively walks a directory and writes eligible files as Markdown sections to the output writer.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if currentDir == null or not a directory, return;
   *   if !optons.recursive and currentDir != baseDir, return;
   *   if optons.ignoreDirs == null, optons.ignoreDirs = createDefaultIgnoreDirs();
   *   directoryFilter.ignoreDirs = optons.ignoreDirs;
   *   currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
   *   if currentDir != baseDir and directoryFilter.isIgnoredPath(currentRel), return;
   *   entries = Files.list(currentDir) sorted lexicographically by file name;
   *   for each entry in entries:
   *       if entry is a directory:
   *           if optons.recursive:
   *               walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
   *       else if entry is a regular file:
   *           rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
   *           if isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.ignoreDirs), continue;
   *           ext = getExtension(entry);
   *           if !shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.fileTypes), continue;
   *           readResult = fileContentReader.readFileSafely(entry, optons.maxFileSizeMb);
   *           if !readResult.readable, continue;
   *           fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.content);
   *           result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
   *           md.write(markdownWriter.formatEntry(fileEntry));
   *           if formatted string does not end with newline, write a newline;
   * Related Features: directoryFilter, fileContentReader, markdownWriter, createDefaultIgnoreDirs, getExtension, shouldIncludeExtension, isIgnored, createMarkdownFileEntry
   * Related Classes: DirectoryFilter, DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, MarkdownWriter, FileContentReader, FileReadResult
   * Input: currentDir - directory to scan; md - BufferedWriter for the Markdown output; optons - DirectoryToMarkdownOptions; baseDir - root directory used to build relative paths; result - MarkdownConversionResult accumulating processed files.
   * Output: void; side effects are writing Markdown to md and adding entries to result.
   * Pre-condition: currentDir, md, baseDir, result, optons, directoryFilter, fileContentReader, and markdownWriter are non-null; md is open and writable.
   * Post-condition: All eligible regular files under currentDir have been written as Markdown sections in stable sorted order; result.processedFileCount is consistent with result.processedFiles.size(); unreadable, too-large, filtered, or ignored files are skipped.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public void walkDirectory(final Path currentDir, final BufferedWriter md, final DirectoryToMarkdownOptions optons, final Path baseDir, final MarkdownConversionResult result)
  {
    if (currentDir == null || !Files.isDirectory(currentDir))
    {
      return;
    }
    if (!optons.isRecursive() && !currentDir.equals(baseDir))
    {
      return;
    }
    if (optons.getIgnoreDirs() == null)
    {
      optons.getIgnoreDirs().addAll(createDefaultIgnoreDirs());
    }
    getDirectoryFilter().getIgnoreDirs().clear();
    getDirectoryFilter().getIgnoreDirs().addAll(optons.getIgnoreDirs());
    Path currentRel = baseDir.relativize(currentDir);
    if (!currentDir.equals(baseDir) && getDirectoryFilter().isIgnoredPath(currentRel))
    {
      return;
    }
    try (Stream<Path> entries = Files.list(currentDir))
    {
      List<Path> sortedEntries = entries
          .sorted(Comparator.comparing(p -> p.getFileName().toString()))
          .collect(Collectors.toList());
      for (Path entry : sortedEntries)
      {
        if (Files.isDirectory(entry))
        {
          if (optons.isRecursive())
          {
            walkDirectory(entry, md, optons, baseDir, result);
          }
        }
        else if (Files.isRegularFile(entry))
        {
          Path rel = baseDir.relativize(entry);
          if (isIgnored(rel.toString(), optons.getIgnoreDirs()))
          {
            continue;
          }
          String ext = getExtension(entry);
          if (!shouldIncludeExtension(ext, optons.getFileTypes()))
          {
            continue;
          }
          FileReadResult readResult = getFileContentReader().readFileSafely(entry, optons.getMaxFileSizeMb());
          if (readResult == null || !readResult.isReadable())
          {
            continue;
          }
          MarkdownFileEntry fileEntry = createMarkdownFileEntry(rel, ext, readResult.getContent());
          result.addProcessedFile(fileEntry);
          String formatted = getMarkdownWriter().formatEntry(fileEntry);
          md.write(formatted);
          if (!formatted.endsWith("\n"))
          {
            md.write("\n");
          }
        }
      }
    }
    catch (IOException e)
    {
      throw new RuntimeException(e);
    }
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Builds a MarkdownFileEntry object for one successfully read file.
   * Algorithm:
   *   ext = extension == null ? "" : extension;
   *   language = ext.startsWith(".") ? ext.substring(1) : ext;
   *   relative = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');
   *   return new MarkdownFileEntry(relative, ext, language, content);
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
   * Input: relativePath - Path of the file relative to the root directory; extension - lowercase file extension including the leading dot, or empty; content - UTF-8 text content of the file.
   * Output: MarkdownFileEntry containing relativePath, extension, language (extension without the leading dot), and content.
   * Pre-condition: relativePath is non-null; extension is non-null; content may be empty but is non-null.
   * Post-condition: The returned entry has language derived from extension and a Markdown-portable relative path using '/' separators.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model relativePathDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public MarkdownFileEntry createMarkdownFileEntry(final Path relativePath, final String extension, final String content)
  {
    String ext = extension == null ? "" : extension;
    String language = ext.startsWith(".") ? ext.substring(1) : ext;
    String relative = relativePath.toString().replace('\\', '/');
    MarkdownFileEntry entry = JosephFactory.eINSTANCE.createMarkdownFileEntry();
    entry.setRelativePath(relative);
    entry.setExtension(ext);
    entry.setLanguage(language);
    entry.setContent(content);
    return entry;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Determines whether a file extension should be included based on the configured file type filter.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if fileTypes == null or fileTypes.isEmpty(), return true;
   *   if extension == null, return false;
   *   ext = extension.toLowerCase();
   *   for each type in fileTypes:
   *       if type != null and type.toLowerCase().equals(ext), return true;
   *   return false;
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: extension - lowercase file extension including leading dot, e.g. ".txt"; fileTypes - list of extensions including leading dot, or null/empty for no filter.
   * Output: boolean - true if the extension is accepted.
   * Pre-condition: extension is non-null, or false is returned when a filter exists.
   * Post-condition: An empty or null filter admits all extensions; otherwise comparison is case-insensitive and requires exact full extension matching.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model fileTypesMany="true"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean shouldIncludeExtension(final String extension, final EList<String> fileTypes)
  {
    if (fileTypes == null || fileTypes.isEmpty())
    {
      return true;
    }
    if (extension == null)
    {
      return false;
    }
    String ext = extension.toLowerCase();
    for (String type : fileTypes)
    {
      if (type != null && type.toLowerCase().equals(ext))
      {
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Returns the lowercase file extension of a path, including the leading dot.
   * Algorithm:
   *   if path == null or path.getFileName() == null, return "";
   *   name = path.getFileName().toString();
   *   lastDot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
   *   if lastDot < 0 || lastDot == 0 || lastDot == name.length()-1, return "";
   *   return name.substring(lastDot).toLowerCase();
   * Related Features: None
   * Related Classes: None
   * Input: path - file or directory Path.
   * Output: String - extension with leading dot, lowercased; empty string if no extension is present.
   * Pre-condition: path is non-null, or empty string is returned.
   * Post-condition: The result does not include the file name before the last dot; hidden dotfiles such as ".gitignore" have no extension.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model pathDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public String getExtension(final Path path)
  {
    if (path == null || path.getFileName() == null)
    {
      return "";
    }
    String name = path.getFileName().toString();
    int lastDot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
    if (lastDot < 0 || lastDot == 0 || lastDot == name.length() - 1)
    {
      return "";
    }
    return name.substring(lastDot).toLowerCase();
  }

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

You will be given a **Java Class** that is originally generated by EMF code generator with `suppressInterfaces=true` and later is completed by LLMs. Your task is to fix the **Compilation Errors** by taking additional contextual information into account.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. Read the error messages carefully.
3. Do your best to fix the errors locally without introducing new types, fields, and methods.
4. You should not remove faulty methods to eliminate compilation errors.
5. Do not change parts without errors.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.
